- **skillops-router**: 面向 skill 体系运维的总入口路由器。用于在技能发现、安装、来源材料抽取成 skill、创建/更新、插件脚手架、以及 skill 使用闭环复盘之间选择最合适的技能；当用户要扩技能体系、整理 skills、决定该扩现有 skill 还是新建 skill，或把人/书/理论/博客蒸馏成 skill 时优先使用。 ([Ocean326/Agents](https://github.com/Ocean326/Agents/tree/HEAD/skills/global/skillops-router))
- **skillops-troubleshoot**: Find why Switch SkillOps is stuck or misbehaving - agents not answering in Mattermost, an agent in the wrong folder, `work.py` refusing a step, a command denied, or a request in an unexpected state. Use when debugging the SkillOps setup or a demo run, not the demo app's code. Not for the skillops-manager, skillops-coder or skillops-reviewer agents; they follow only their CLAUDE.md. ([gaikwadashutosh35/switch-skillops](https://github.com/gaikwadashutosh35/switch-skillops/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/skillops-troubleshoot))
- **skillopt**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/bash/skillopt) · ★745)
- **skillopt**: Runs the SkillOpt single-lineage optimization loop, which organizes a hill-climb into epochs over mini-batches of train tasks under a textual learning rate — an integer edit budget that decays on a constant|linear|cosine schedule — and ends each epoch with one extra gated consolidation step. Parent is always the current best; acceptance is the val significance gate. Use when a run should anneal from broad early edits to small late ones and consolidate once per epoch, rather than hill-climb's one-shot whole-trainset proposals or gepa's Pareto frontier. ([skillberry-ai/cap-evolve](https://github.com/skillberry-ai/cap-evolve/tree/HEAD/skills/algorithms/skillopt) · ★70)
- **skillopt**: Improve a skill, agent file, or prompt through bounded patches and a held-out, per-task acceptance gate. Use when you say `optimize this skill`, `make this skill better`, `compress these skills safely`, `tune this prompt against an eval`, or a skill keeps failing the same way. Do NOT use to create or route skills (use skillforge) or to decide skill placement or compress markdown without an eval (use context-optimizer). ([rjmurillo/ai-agents](https://github.com/rjmurillo/ai-agents/tree/HEAD/src/cursor/skills/skillopt) · ★49 · 6 copies)
- **skillopt**: Prepare or run Whetstone SkillOpt optimization when asked to optimize a process skill using SkillOpt. ([iliaal/whetstone](https://github.com/iliaal/whetstone/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/skillopt) · ★37)
- **skillopt**: Bounded text-space gradient optimization for SKILL.md prompts using execution traces. Loads low-reward traces (reward &lt; 0.8) from .agent/traces/, groups them by skill, and applies bounded edits (max 15% word change) with a Validation Gate to prevent destructive rewrites. Use when you want to run the SkillOpt algorithm to automatically improve skill prompts, or when a skill repeatedly underperforms and you want a systematic text-space optimization pass. Triggers: skillopt, skill optimization, bounded edits, prompt gradient, validation gate, improve skill ([LowyShin/giip-fde-agent](https://github.com/LowyShin/giip-fde-agent/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/skillopt) · ★34)
- **skillopt**: Run controlled skill optimization cycles on any skill document. Uses kanban-based pipelines with validation gates — the methodology from Microsoft Research's SkillOpt (arXiv 2605.23904). ([magnus919/hermes-SkillOpt](https://github.com/magnus919/hermes-SkillOpt/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★32 · 2 copies)

([fj906880l-web/web-and-bios-scrapper-](https://github.com/fj906880l-web/web-and-bios-scrapper-/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ops/modules/skillopt))
- **skillopt**: Meta-skill that wraps every other skill invocation with the SkillOpt discipline (Yang et al., Microsoft + SJTU + Tongji + Fudan, May 2026, arXiv:2605.23904v2). Treats each skill document as the trainable external state of a frozen agent and uses bounded add/delete/replace edits, a held-out validation gate, a rejected-edit buffer, and an epoch-wise slow/meta update to improve the skill over time. Load this skill on every future skill call — whenever the agent loads any user, space, org, or built-in skill, also load skillopt and run the SkillOpt loop around the target skill's execution. - **skills**: Meta-skill for a skill system — the operating principle that anything an agent should know how to do quickly and easily is encoded as a skill, and the mechanics of how to build one.
